<script type="text/javascript" src="/Scripts/common.js?v=1.2"></script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    $(function () {
        $(".countydata").click(function () {

            
            var electionID = localStorage.getItem('ElectionId') || readQueryStringValue('ELECTIONID');
            var electionType = localStorage.getItem('ElectionType') || readQueryStringValue('ElectionType');

            var isActive = localStorage.getItem('IsActive') || readQueryStringValue('IsActive')|| window.sessionStorage.getItem('IsActive');
            var officeId = localStorage.getItem('officeId') || readQueryStringValue('officeId');
            var districtName = localStorage.getItem('districtName') || readQueryStringValue('districtname')|| window.sessionStorage.getItem('districtName');

            var districtId = localStorage.getItem('districtId') || sessionStorage.getItem('DistrictId') || readQueryStringValue('districtId') || 1;

            window.localStorage['currentURL'] = window.location.href;
            const countyName = this.text.toUpperCase() === 'MCKEAN' ? 'McKEAN' : this.text.toUpperCase();
            const x = encodeURI(getRedirectURL(window.location.href, applicationPath, countyName, electionID, electionType, isActive, districtId, officeId, 'countymenu', districtName));
            console.log('redirecting to ' + x);
            window.location.href = x;

        });

        function getAllQueryStringKeys(paraName) {
            const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            const keys ='';

            for (const key of urlParams.keys()) {
                if (key.toLocaleLowerCase() == paraName.toLocaleLowerCase())
                    return key;
            }

            return paraName;
        }

        function readQueryStringValue(paraName) {
            
            var keys = getAllQueryStringKeys(paraName);
            var urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            const myParam = urlParams.get(keys);
            return myParam;
        }

    });

    $(document).ready(function () {

        function resetCountySMenuToggle() {
            $('.dropdown a.toggleStop').each(function (index, value) {
                if ($(this).next('ul').css('display') === 'block') {
                    $(this).next('ul').toggle();
                }
            });

          
        }
        function resetAllMenuToggle() {
            $('.dropdown').each(function (item, index) {
                $(this).find('.dropdown-menu').each(function (item1, index) {

                    if ($(this).css('display') === 'block') {
                       // $(this).toggle();
                        $(this).removeAttr('style');
                    }
                })
            })
        }

       

        function resetMenuToggle(currObj) {
            $(currObj).each(function (item, index) {
                $(this).find('ul').each(function (item1, index) {
                    if ($(this).css('display') === 'block') {
                        //$(this).toggle();
                        $(this).removeAttr('style');
                    }
                })
            })
        }


        

        $('.menu').on('click', '> li', function (event) {
            var submenu = $(this).children('ul');
            if (submenu.length) {
                submenu.slideToggle();
                $(this).toggleClass('open');
            }
            event.stopPropagation(); // Stop the event from bubbling up to the document
        });

        // Event delegation for inner list items
        $('.menu').on('click', 'li ul li', function (event) {
            event.stopPropagation(); // Prevents the click event from bubbling up
            var subsubmenu = $(this).children('ul');
            if (subsubmenu.length) {
                subsubmenu.slideToggle();
                $(this).toggleClass('open');
            }
        });

        function isValidHttpUrl(string) {
            try {
                const newUrl = new URL(string);
                return newUrl.protocol === 'http:' || newUrl.protocol === 'https:';
            }
            catch (err) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        function attributeEncode(str) {
            return str.replace(/["&'<>]/g, function (char) {
                switch (char) {
                    case '"': return '&quot;';
                    case '&': return '&amp;';
                    case "'": return '&#39;';
                    case '<': return '&lt;';
                    case '>': return '&gt;';
                }
            });
        }

        $('.dropdown-toggle').on('keydown', function (e) {
            var $this = $(this);
            var $parent = $this.closest('li');
            var $submenu = $parent.find('.dropdown-menu').first();

            switch (e.key) {
                case "Tab":
                    if ($('li.dropdown').hasClass('open')) {
                        var obj = $('li.dropdown.open a.dropdown-toggle');
                        if (obj.length > 0) {
                            obj.dropdown('toggle');
                            obj.parent().removeClass('open');

                        }
                    } else {
                        resetAllMenuToggle();
                    }
                    break;
                case 'Escape':
                    
                    resetAllMenuToggle();

                    if ($('li.dropdown').hasClass('open')) {
                        var obj = $('li.dropdown.open a.dropdown-toggle');
                        if (obj.length > 0) {
                            obj.dropdown('toggle');
                            obj.parent().removeClass('open');

                        }
                    }
                    e.stopPropagation();

                    break;
                case 'ArrowDown':
                    e.preventDefault();

                    if (!$parent.hasClass('open')) {
                        if ($this.hasClass('dropdown-toggle')) {
                            $this.dropdown('toggle');
                            $submenu.find('li:first-child > a').focus();
                        }
                    } else {
                        $submenu.find('li:first-child > a').focus();
                    }
                    break;
                case 'ArrowRight':
                    e.preventDefault();

                    if (($submenu || '').length > 0) {
                        $submenu.toggle();
                        $submenu.find('li:first-child > a').focus();
                    }

                    break;
                case 'ArrowLeft':
                    e.preventDefault();
                    if ($parent.hasClass('open')) {
                        $parent.removeClass('open');
                        resetMenuToggle($parent);
                        $parent.closest('.dropdown-menu').prev('a').focus();
                    }
                    break;
                case 'Enter':
                    e.preventDefault();
                    if ($this.attr('target') === '_blank') {
                        const url = encodeURI($this.attr('href'));
                        const urlencoded = attributeEncode(url);
                        if (isValidHttpUrl(urlencoded)) {
                            $this.click();
                        }
                    } else {
                        
                        var nextSubMenu = $this.next('ul.dropdown-menu');
                        if ((nextSubMenu || '').length <= 0) {
                            event = 'enter';
                            $this.click();
                        } else {
                            if (($submenu || '').length > 0) {
                                // resetCountySMenuToggle();
                                $submenu.toggle();
                                $submenu.find('li:first-child > a').focus();
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    break;
            }
        });


       

        var event = '';
        $(document).on('click', function (a) {
            if (event !== 'enter') {
                var $this = $(this);
                resetAllMenuToggle();
               
            } else {
                event = '';
            }
        });
      

        $(document).on('keydown', '.dropdown-menu a', function (e) {
            var $this = $(this);
            var $parentLi = $this.parent('li');
            var $submenu = $parentLi.find('.dropdown-menu').first();

            switch (e.key) {
                case "Tab":
                    // e.preventDefault();
                    if ($('li.dropdown').hasClass('open')) {
                        var obj = $('li.dropdown.open a.dropdown-toggle');
                        if (obj.length > 0) {
                            obj.dropdown('toggle');
                            obj.parent().removeClass('open');
                        }
                    }
                    //} else {
                    //    resetAllMenuToggle();
                    //}
                    resetAllMenuToggle();

                    break;
                case 'Escape':
                    
                    if ($('li.dropdown').hasClass('open')) {
                        var obj = $('li.dropdown.open a.dropdown-toggle');
                        if (obj.length > 0) {
                            obj.parent().removeClass('open');

                        }
                        e.preventDefault();
                    } 

                    resetAllMenuToggle();
                    break;
                case 'ArrowDown':
                    e.preventDefault();
                    if ($parentLi.next('li').length) {
                        $parentLi.next('li').find('a').first().focus();
                    } else {
                        $parentLi.siblings('li').first().find('a').first().focus();
                    }
                    break;
                case 'ArrowUp':
                    e.preventDefault();
                    if ($parentLi.prev('li').length) {
                        $parentLi.prev('li').find('a').first().focus();
                    } else {
                        $parentLi.siblings('li').last().find('a').first().focus();
                    }
                    break;
                case 'ArrowRight':
                    e.preventDefault();

                    if (($submenu || '').length > 0) {
                        // resetCountySMenuToggle();
                        $submenu.toggle();
                        $submenu.find('li:first-child > a').focus();
                    }
                    break;
                case 'ArrowLeft':
                    e.preventDefault();
                    if ($parentLi.closest('.dropdown-menu').parent('li').hasClass('dropdown-submenu')) {
                        resetMenuToggle($parentLi.closest('.dropdown-menu').parent('li'));
                        $parentLi.closest('.dropdown-menu').prev('a').focus();
                    }
                    break;

                case 'Enter':
                    e.preventDefault();
                    if ($this.attr('target') === '_blank') {
                        const url = encodeURI($this.attr('href'));
                        const urlencoded = attributeEncode(url);
                        if (isValidHttpUrl(urlencoded)) {
                            $this.click();
                        }
                    } else {
                        //event = 'enter'
                        //$this.click();
                        var nextSubMenu = $this.next('ul.dropdown-menu');
                        if ((nextSubMenu || '').length <= 0) { $this.click(); } else {
                            if (($submenu || '').length > 0) {
                                // resetCountySMenuToggle();
                                $submenu.toggle();
                                $submenu.find('li:first-child > a').focus();
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    break;
                default:
                    resetAllMenuToggle();
                    if ($('.open').length > 0) {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        resetCountySMenuToggle();
                        var obj = $('li.dropdown.open a.dropdown-toggle');
                        if (obj.length > 0) {
                            obj.dropdown('toggle');
                        }
                    }
                    break;
            }
        });

    });

    $("#expandMobileMene").click(function () {
        $('#expandMenu').show();
        //$(".overlay").show();
    });
    $("#closeMobileMenu").click(function () {
        $('#expandMenu').hide();
        //$(".overlay").hide();
    });
</script>
            </header>

            <!-- Main Body Section with ARIA Landmark and Region Management -->
            <main class="body" role="main" aria-labelledby="main-content">
                

<style>
    panel-body-party {
        padding-left: 0px !important;
    }

    h5.boxtitle {
        display: flex;
        font-size: 16px !important;
    }

        h5.boxtitle span:nth-child(1) {
            /*order: 1; /* Span 1 on the left */
            flex-grow: 0;
            flex-shrink: 0;
            width: 40%

<script type="text/javascript" src="/Scripts/jquery.maphilight.js?v=1.2"></script>
<script type="text/javascript" src="/Scripts/imageMapResizer.js?v=1.2"></script>
<script type="text/javascript" src="/Scripts/common.js?v=1.2"></script>
<script type="text/javascript">

    $(function () {
        var currentIndex = -1;
        var $dropdownLinks = $("#dropdownCnt-content a");
        $("map area").attr("tabindex", "-1");
        var csearchVal = localStorage.getItem('countySearch');
        if (csearchVal !== '') {
            filterDropdown(csearchVal);
        }

        var counties;
        var countiesJson = fetch('../Scripts/Counties.json')
            .then(res => res.json())
            .then(data => {
                counties = data;
            })
            .catch(error => {
            });

        $('#countyName').addClass('badge badge-pill badge-primary selectedCounty')

        $('#filterByLabel').on('click', function () { });

        $('#expand').on('click', function () {

            var electionType = localStorage.getItem('ElectionType');
            var electionID = localStorage.getItem('ElectionId');
            var isActive = localStorage.getItem('IsActive');
            var officeId = localStorage.getItem('officeId');
            var districtId = localStorage.getItem('districtId') || 1;

            const baseURL = window.location.origin;
            window.localStorage['currentURL'] = window.location.href;
            console.log('baseURL =' + baseURL);
            var url = baseURL + '/Home/ShowExpandedMap?districtId=' + districtId + '&officeId=' + officeId + '&electionType=' + electionType + '&electionID=' + electionID
                + "&isActive=" + isActive + '&applicationPath=' + baseURL; // URL you want to open

            //console.log(url);

            document.querySelector(".popup-window iframe").setAttribute('src', url);
            $('.overlay, .popup-window').fadeIn();

            // Attach keydown event listener for Escape key to close the popup
            $(document).on('keydown', function (e) {
                if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                    $('.overlay, .popup-window').fadeOut();
                    $(document).off('keydown');
                }
            });
        });

        $('.close-popup').on('click', function () {
            $('.overlay, .popup-window').fadeOut();
        });

        window.addEventListener('message', function (event) {
            if (event.origin !== window.location.origin) {
                return;
            }

            if (event.data.type === 'redirect') {
                const url = encodeURI(event.data.url);
                window.location.href = url;
            }
        });

        function filterDropdown(value) {

            $("#dropdownCnt-content a").filter(function (index) {
                $(this).toggle(
                    $(this).text().toLowerCase().indexOf(value) > -1 &&
                    (value !== '' || index < 68)
                );
            });
            $("#dropdownCnt-content").show();
        }

        $("#searchInput").on("keyup", function (e) {
            var value = $(this).val().toLowerCase();
            filterDropdown(value);

            if (e.key === "ArrowDown" || e.key === "ArrowUp") {
                e.preventDefault();
                ensureItemVisible(navigateDropdown(e.key));

            } else if (e.key === "Enter" && currentIndex > -1) {
                e.preventDefault();
                $("#dropdownCnt-content a:visible").eq(currentIndex).click();

                var val = $("#dropdownCnt-content a:visible").eq(currentIndex).text();
                var idx = counties.indexOf(val);

                if (idx >= 0) {
                    localStorage.setItem('countySearch', counties[idx]);
                }
            }
        });

        function navigateDropdown(key) {
            var visibleLinks = $dropdownLinks.filter(':visible');
            //console.log('visibleLinks')
            //console.log(visibleLinks.length)
            if (key === "ArrowDown") {
                currentIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % visibleLinks.length;
                //console.log(currentIndex)
            } else if (key === "ArrowUp") {
                currentIndex = (currentIndex - 1 + visibleLinks.length) % visibleLinks.length;
            }
            visibleLinks.removeClass('highlight');
            return visibleLinks.eq(currentIndex).addClass('highlight');
        }

        $("#searchInput").on("click", function () {
            filterDropdown('');
        });

        // Close dropdown on Escape key press
        $("#searchInput").on("keydown", function (e) {
            console.log(e.key)
            if (e.key === "Escape") {
                $(this).val('');
                $(this).blur();
                $("#dropdownCnt-content").hide();
                localStorage.setItem('countySearch', null);
            }
        });

        $("#dropdownCnt-content a").on("keydown", function (e) {

            if (e.key === "Escape") {
                $("#searchInput").val('');
                $(this).val('');
                $(this).blur();
                currentIndex = -1;
                $("#dropdownCnt-content").hide();
            }
        });

        $("#dropdownCnt-content a").on("click", function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            var selectedValue = $(this).data("value");
            var sval = $("#searchInput").val();

            var idx = counties.indexOf(sval);

            if (idx >= 0) {
                localStorage.setItem('countySearch', counties[idx]);
            }

            changeCount(selectedValue);
            $("#dropdownCnt-content").hide();
            $("#searchInput").val($(this).text());
            currentIndex = -1;

        });

        $(document).on("click", function (e) {
            if (!$(e.target).closest(".dropdownCnt").length) {
                $("#dropdownCnt-content").hide();
            }
        });

        $('map').imageMapResize();

        $(".maparea").click(function () {
            changeCount(this.alt);

        });

        $("#county").change(function () {
            changeCount($(this).val());
        });

        function getAllQueryStringKeys(paraName) {
            const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            const keys = '';

            for (const key of urlParams.keys()) {
                if (key.toLocaleLowerCase() == paraName.toLocaleLowerCase())
                    return key;
            }

            return paraName;
        }

        function readQueryStringValue(paraName) {

            var keys = getAllQueryStringKeys(paraName);
            var urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            const myParam = urlParams.get(keys);
            return myParam;
        }

        function readVariableValue(object, key) {
            for (let a in object) {
                //console.log(sessionStorage[a]);
                if (a.toLocaleLowerCase() == key.toLocaleLowerCase())
                    return object[a];
            }
        }

        function changeCount(countyName) {
            console.clear();
            console.log("changeCount")
            var electionType = localStorage.getItem('ElectionType') || readQueryStringValue('ElectionType');
            var electionID = localStorage.getItem('ElectionId') || readQueryStringValue('ElectionID');
            var isActive = localStorage.getItem('IsActive') || readQueryStringValue('IsActive');
            var officeId = localStorage.getItem('officeId');
            if ((officeId || 'undefined') == 'undefined' || (officeId || '') == '')
                officeId = readVariableValue(sessionStorage, 'OfficeId') || readQueryStringValue('OfficeId');
            // var officeId = localStorage.getItem('officeId') || sessionStorage.getItem('OfficeId') || readQueryStringValue('OfficeId');
            var districtId = localStorage.getItem('districtId') || sessionStorage.getItem('DistrictId') || readQueryStringValue('DistrictId') || 1;

        }

        function ensureItemVisible(item) {
            var dropdownContent = $('.dropdownCnt-content');
            var scrollTop = dropdownContent.scrollTop();
            var dropdownOffsetTop = dropdownContent.offset().top; // Top position of the dropdown container
            var dropdownHeight = dropdownContent.height();
            var itemOffsetTop = item.offset().top - dropdownOffsetTop; // Corrected item top position
            var itemHeight = item.outerHeight();

            if (itemOffsetTop < 0) {
                // Scroll up to make the item visible at the top
                dropdownContent.scrollTop(scrollTop + itemOffsetTop);
            } else if (itemOffsetTop + itemHeight > dropdownHeight) {
                // Scroll down to make the item visible at the bottom
                dropdownContent.scrollTop(scrollTop + (itemOffsetTop - dropdownHeight + itemHeight));
            }
        }
    });

    $(window).on('load', function () {
        $('#filterByLabel').trigger('click');
    });
</script>

<script type="text/javascript" src="/Scripts/common.js?v=1.2"></script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    $(function () {
        $(".countydata").click(function () {

            
            var electionID = localStorage.getItem('ElectionId') || readQueryStringValue('ELECTIONID');
            var electionType = localStorage.getItem('ElectionType') || readQueryStringValue('ElectionType');

            var isActive = localStorage.getItem('IsActive') || readQueryStringValue('IsActive')|| window.sessionStorage.getItem('IsActive');
            var officeId = localStorage.getItem('officeId') || readQueryStringValue('officeId');
            var districtName = localStorage.getItem('districtName') || readQueryStringValue('districtname')|| window.sessionStorage.getItem('districtName');

            var districtId = localStorage.getItem('districtId') || sessionStorage.getItem('DistrictId') || readQueryStringValue('districtId') || 1;

            window.localStorage['currentURL'] = window.location.href;
            const countyName = this.text.toUpperCase() === 'MCKEAN' ? 'McKEAN' : this.text.toUpperCase();
            const x = encodeURI(getRedirectURL(window.location.href, applicationPath, countyName, electionID, electionType, isActive, districtId, officeId, 'countymenu', districtName));
            console.log('redirecting to ' + x);
            window.location.href = x;

        });

        function getAllQueryStringKeys(paraName) {
            const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            const keys ='';

            for (const key of urlParams.keys()) {
                if (key.toLocaleLowerCase() == paraName.toLocaleLowerCase())
                    return key;
            }

            return paraName;
        }

        function readQueryStringValue(paraName) {
            
            var keys = getAllQueryStringKeys(paraName);
            var urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            const myParam = urlParams.get(keys);
            return myParam;
        }

    });

    $(document).ready(function () {

        function resetCountySMenuToggle() {
            $('.dropdown a.toggleStop').each(function (index, value) {
                if ($(this).next('ul').css('display') === 'block') {
                    $(this).next('ul').toggle();
                }
            });

          
        }
        function resetAllMenuToggle() {
            $('.dropdown').each(function (item, index) {
                $(this).find('.dropdown-menu').each(function (item1, index) {

                    if ($(this).css('display') === 'block') {
                       // $(this).toggle();
                        $(this).removeAttr('style');
                    }
                })
            })
        }

       

        function resetMenuToggle(currObj) {
            $(currObj).each(function (item, index) {
                $(this).find('ul').each(function (item1, index) {
                    if ($(this).css('display') === 'block') {
                        //$(this).toggle();
                        $(this).removeAttr('style');
                    }
                })
            })
        }


        

        $('.menu').on('click', '> li', function (event) {
            var submenu = $(this).children('ul');
            if (submenu.length) {
                submenu.slideToggle();
                $(this).toggleClass('open');
            }
            event.stopPropagation(); // Stop the event from bubbling up to the document
        });

        // Event delegation for inner list items
        $('.menu').on('click', 'li ul li', function (event) {
            event.stopPropagation(); // Prevents the click event from bubbling up
            var subsubmenu = $(this).children('ul');
            if (subsubmenu.length) {
                subsubmenu.slideToggle();
                $(this).toggleClass('open');
            }
        });

        function isValidHttpUrl(string) {
            try {
                const newUrl = new URL(string);
                return newUrl.protocol === 'http:' || newUrl.protocol === 'https:';
            }
            catch (err) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        function attributeEncode(str) {
            return str.replace(/["&'<>]/g, function (char) {
                switch (char) {
                    case '"': return '&quot;';
                    case '&': return '&amp;';
                    case "'": return '&#39;';
                    case '<': return '&lt;';
                    case '>': return '&gt;';
                }
            });
        }

        $('.dropdown-toggle').on('keydown', function (e) {
            var $this = $(this);
            var $parent = $this.closest('li');
            var $submenu = $parent.find('.dropdown-menu').first();

            switch (e.key) {
                case "Tab":
                    if ($('li.dropdown').hasClass('open')) {
                        var obj = $('li.dropdown.open a.dropdown-toggle');
                        if (obj.length > 0) {
                            obj.dropdown('toggle');
                            obj.parent().removeClass('open');

                        }
                    } else {
                        resetAllMenuToggle();
                    }
                    break;
                case 'Escape':
                    
                    resetAllMenuToggle();

                    if ($('li.dropdown').hasClass('open')) {
                        var obj = $('li.dropdown.open a.dropdown-toggle');
                        if (obj.length > 0) {
                            obj.dropdown('toggle');
                            obj.parent().removeClass('open');

                        }
                    }
                    e.stopPropagation();

                    break;
                case 'ArrowDown':
                    e.preventDefault();

                    if (!$parent.hasClass('open')) {
                        if ($this.hasClass('dropdown-toggle')) {
                            $this.dropdown('toggle');
                            $submenu.find('li:first-child > a').focus();
                        }
                    } else {
                        $submenu.find('li:first-child > a').focus();
                    }
                    break;
                case 'ArrowRight':
                    e.preventDefault();

                    if (($submenu || '').length > 0) {
                        $submenu.toggle();
                        $submenu.find('li:first-child > a').focus();
                    }

                    break;
                case 'ArrowLeft':
                    e.preventDefault();
                    if ($parent.hasClass('open')) {
                        $parent.removeClass('open');
                        resetMenuToggle($parent);
                        $parent.closest('.dropdown-menu').prev('a').focus();
                    }
                    break;
                case 'Enter':
                    e.preventDefault();
                    if ($this.attr('target') === '_blank') {
                        const url = encodeURI($this.attr('href'));
                        const urlencoded = attributeEncode(url);
                        if (isValidHttpUrl(urlencoded)) {
                            $this.click();
                        }
                    } else {
                        
                        var nextSubMenu = $this.next('ul.dropdown-menu');
                        if ((nextSubMenu || '').length <= 0) {
                            event = 'enter';
                            $this.click();
                        } else {
                            if (($submenu || '').length > 0) {
                                // resetCountySMenuToggle();
                                $submenu.toggle();
                                $submenu.find('li:first-child > a').focus();
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    break;
            }
        });


       

        var event = '';
        $(document).on('click', function (a) {
            if (event !== 'enter') {
                var $this = $(this);
                resetAllMenuToggle();
               
            } else {
                event = '';
            }
        });
      

        $(document).on('keydown', '.dropdown-menu a', function (e) {
            var $this = $(this);
            var $parentLi = $this.parent('li');
            var $submenu = $parentLi.find('.dropdown-menu').first();

            switch (e.key) {
                case "Tab":
                    // e.preventDefault();
                    if ($('li.dropdown').hasClass('open')) {
                        var obj = $('li.dropdown.open a.dropdown-toggle');
                        if (obj.length > 0) {
                            obj.dropdown('toggle');
                            obj.parent().removeClass('open');
                        }
                    }
                    //} else {
                    //    resetAllMenuToggle();
                    //}
                    resetAllMenuToggle();

                    break;
                case 'Escape':
                    
                    if ($('li.dropdown').hasClass('open')) {
                        var obj = $('li.dropdown.open a.dropdown-toggle');
                        if (obj.length > 0) {
                            obj.parent().removeClass('open');

                        }
                        e.preventDefault();
                    } 

                    resetAllMenuToggle();
                    break;
                case 'ArrowDown':
                    e.preventDefault();
                    if ($parentLi.next('li').length) {
                        $parentLi.next('li').find('a').first().focus();
                    } else {
                        $parentLi.siblings('li').first().find('a').first().focus();
                    }
                    break;
                case 'ArrowUp':
                    e.preventDefault();
                    if ($parentLi.prev('li').length) {
                        $parentLi.prev('li').find('a').first().focus();
                    } else {
                        $parentLi.siblings('li').last().find('a').first().focus();
                    }
                    break;
                case 'ArrowRight':
                    e.preventDefault();

                    if (($submenu || '').length > 0) {
                        // resetCountySMenuToggle();
                        $submenu.toggle();
                        $submenu.find('li:first-child > a').focus();
                    }
                    break;
                case 'ArrowLeft':
                    e.preventDefault();
                    if ($parentLi.closest('.dropdown-menu').parent('li').hasClass('dropdown-submenu')) {
                        resetMenuToggle($parentLi.closest('.dropdown-menu').parent('li'));
                        $parentLi.closest('.dropdown-menu').prev('a').focus();
                    }
                    break;

                case 'Enter':
                    e.preventDefault();
                    if ($this.attr('target') === '_blank') {
                        const url = encodeURI($this.attr('href'));
                        const urlencoded = attributeEncode(url);
                        if (isValidHttpUrl(urlencoded)) {
                            $this.click();
                        }
                    } else {
                        //event = 'enter'
                        //$this.click();
                        var nextSubMenu = $this.next('ul.dropdown-menu');
                        if ((nextSubMenu || '').length <= 0) { $this.click(); } else {
                            if (($submenu || '').length > 0) {
                                // resetCountySMenuToggle();
                                $submenu.toggle();
                                $submenu.find('li:first-child > a').focus();
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    break;
                default:
                    resetAllMenuToggle();
                    if ($('.open').length > 0) {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        resetCountySMenuToggle();
                        var obj = $('li.dropdown.open a.dropdown-toggle');
                        if (obj.length > 0) {
                            obj.dropdown('toggle');
                        }
                    }
                    break;
            }
        });

    });

    $("#expandMobileMene").click(function () {
        $('#expandMenu').show();
        //$(".overlay").show();
    });
    $("#closeMobileMenu").click(function () {
        $('#expandMenu').hide();
        //$(".overlay").hide();
    });
</script>

                    <style>
    .popup-window {
        display: none;
        position: fixed;
        top: 50%;
        left: 50%;

<script type="text/javascript" src="/Scripts/jquery.maphilight.js?v=1.2"></script>
<script type="text/javascript" src="/Scripts/imageMapResizer.js?v=1.2"></script>
<script type="text/javascript" src="/Scripts/common.js?v=1.2"></script>
<script type="text/javascript">

    $(function () {
        var currentIndex = -1;
        var $dropdownLinks = $("#dropdownCnt-content a");
        $("map area").attr("tabindex", "-1");
        var csearchVal = localStorage.getItem('countySearch');
        if (csearchVal !== '') {
            filterDropdown(csearchVal);
        }

        var counties;
        var countiesJson = fetch('../Scripts/Counties.json')
            .then(res => res.json())
            .then(data => {
                counties = data;
            })
            .catch(error => {
            });

        $('#countyName').addClass('badge badge-pill badge-primary selectedCounty')

        $('#filterByLabel').on('click', function () { });

        $('#expand').on('click', function () {

            var electionType = localStorage.getItem('ElectionType');
            var electionID = localStorage.getItem('ElectionId');
            var isActive = localStorage.getItem('IsActive');
            var officeId = localStorage.getItem('officeId');
            var districtId = localStorage.getItem('districtId') || 1;

            const baseURL = window.location.origin;
            window.localStorage['currentURL'] = window.location.href;
            console.log('baseURL =' + baseURL);
            var url = baseURL + '/Home/ShowExpandedMap?districtId=' + districtId + '&officeId=' + officeId + '&electionType=' + electionType + '&electionID=' + electionID
                + "&isActive=" + isActive + '&applicationPath=' + baseURL; // URL you want to open

            //console.log(url);

            document.querySelector(".popup-window iframe").setAttribute('src', url);
            $('.overlay, .popup-window').fadeIn();

            // Attach keydown event listener for Escape key to close the popup
            $(document).on('keydown', function (e) {
                if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                    $('.overlay, .popup-window').fadeOut();
                    $(document).off('keydown');
                }
            });
        });

        $('.close-popup').on('click', function () {
            $('.overlay, .popup-window').fadeOut();
        });

        window.addEventListener('message', function (event) {
            if (event.origin !== window.location.origin) {
                return;
            }

            if (event.data.type === 'redirect') {
                const url = encodeURI(event.data.url);
                window.location.href = url;
            }
        });

        function filterDropdown(value) {

            $("#dropdownCnt-content a").filter(function (index) {
                $(this).toggle(
                    $(this).text().toLowerCase().indexOf(value) > -1 &&
                    (value !== '' || index < 68)
                );
            });
            $("#dropdownCnt-content").show();
        }

        $("#searchInput").on("keyup", function (e) {
            var value = $(this).val().toLowerCase();
            filterDropdown(value);

            if (e.key === "ArrowDown" || e.key === "ArrowUp") {
                e.preventDefault();
                ensureItemVisible(navigateDropdown(e.key));

            } else if (e.key === "Enter" && currentIndex > -1) {
                e.preventDefault();
                $("#dropdownCnt-content a:visible").eq(currentIndex).click();

                var val = $("#dropdownCnt-content a:visible").eq(currentIndex).text();
                var idx = counties.indexOf(val);

                if (idx >= 0) {
                    localStorage.setItem('countySearch', counties[idx]);
                }
            }
        });

        function navigateDropdown(key) {
            var visibleLinks = $dropdownLinks.filter(':visible');
            //console.log('visibleLinks')
            //console.log(visibleLinks.length)
            if (key === "ArrowDown") {
                currentIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % visibleLinks.length;
                //console.log(currentIndex)
            } else if (key === "ArrowUp") {
                currentIndex = (currentIndex - 1 + visibleLinks.length) % visibleLinks.length;
            }
            visibleLinks.removeClass('highlight');
            return visibleLinks.eq(currentIndex).addClass('highlight');
        }

        $("#searchInput").on("click", function () {
            filterDropdown('');
        });

        // Close dropdown on Escape key press
        $("#searchInput").on("keydown", function (e) {
            console.log(e.key)
            if (e.key === "Escape") {
                $(this).val('');
                $(this).blur();
                $("#dropdownCnt-content").hide();
                localStorage.setItem('countySearch', null);
            }
        });

        $("#dropdownCnt-content a").on("keydown", function (e) {

            if (e.key === "Escape") {
                $("#searchInput").val('');
                $(this).val('');
                $(this).blur();
                currentIndex = -1;
                $("#dropdownCnt-content").hide();
            }
        });

        $("#dropdownCnt-content a").on("click", function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            var selectedValue = $(this).data("value");
            var sval = $("#searchInput").val();

            var idx = counties.indexOf(sval);

            if (idx >= 0) {
                localStorage.setItem('countySearch', counties[idx]);
            }

            changeCount(selectedValue);
            $("#dropdownCnt-content").hide();
            $("#searchInput").val($(this).text());
            currentIndex = -1;

        });

        $(document).on("click", function (e) {
            if (!$(e.target).closest(".dropdownCnt").length) {
                $("#dropdownCnt-content").hide();
            }
        });

        $('map').imageMapResize();

        $(".maparea").click(function () {
            changeCount(this.alt);

        });

        $("#county").change(function () {
            changeCount($(this).val());
        });

        function getAllQueryStringKeys(paraName) {
            const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            const keys = '';

            for (const key of urlParams.keys()) {
                if (key.toLocaleLowerCase() == paraName.toLocaleLowerCase())
                    return key;
            }

            return paraName;
        }

        function readQueryStringValue(paraName) {

            var keys = getAllQueryStringKeys(paraName);
            var urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            const myParam = urlParams.get(keys);
            return myParam;
        }

        function readVariableValue(object, key) {
            for (let a in object) {
                //console.log(sessionStorage[a]);
                if (a.toLocaleLowerCase() == key.toLocaleLowerCase())
                    return object[a];
            }
        }

        function changeCount(countyName) {
            console.clear();
            console.log("changeCount")
            var electionType = localStorage.getItem('ElectionType') || readQueryStringValue('ElectionType');
            var electionID = localStorage.getItem('ElectionId') || readQueryStringValue('ElectionID');
            var isActive = localStorage.getItem('IsActive') || readQueryStringValue('IsActive');
            var officeId = localStorage.getItem('officeId');
            if ((officeId || 'undefined') == 'undefined' || (officeId || '') == '')
                officeId = readVariableValue(sessionStorage, 'OfficeId') || readQueryStringValue('OfficeId');
            // var officeId = localStorage.getItem('officeId') || sessionStorage.getItem('OfficeId') || readQueryStringValue('OfficeId');
            var districtId = localStorage.getItem('districtId') || sessionStorage.getItem('DistrictId') || readQueryStringValue('DistrictId') || 1;
            var districtName = localStorage.getItem('districtName') || readQueryStringValue('districtName') || sessionStorage.getItem('districtName');

            console.log(electionID)
            console.log(isActive)
            console.log(officeId)
            console.log(districtId)

            countyName_ = countyName.toUpperCase();
            window.localStorage['currentURL'] = window.location.href;

            const url = encodeURI(getRedirectURL(window.location.href, applicationPath, countyName_, electionID, electionType, isActive, districtId, officeId, 'countymap', districtName));
            console.log(url)
            window.location.href = url;

            //if (electionType == 'P') {
            //    window.location.href = applicationPath + "Home/CountyResults?countyName=" + countyName + '&ElectionID=' + electionID + '&ElectionType=' + electionType + '&IsActive=' + isActive;
            //} else if (electionType == 'G') {
            //    window.location.href = applicationPath + "General/CountyResults?countyName=" + countyName + '&ElectionID=' + electionID + '&ElectionType=' + electionType + '&IsActive=' + isActive;
            //}
            //else if (electionType == 'S') {
            //    window.location.href = applicationPath + "Special/CountyResults?countyName=" + countyName + '&ElectionID=' + electionID + '&ElectionType=' + electionType + '&IsActive=' + isActive;
            //}
        }

        function ensureItemVisible(item) {
            var dropdownContent = $('.dropdownCnt-content');
            var scrollTop = dropdownContent.scrollTop();
            var dropdownOffsetTop = dropdownContent.offset().top; // Top position of the dropdown container
            var dropdownHeight = dropdownContent.height();
            var itemOffsetTop = item.offset().top - dropdownOffsetTop; // Corrected item top position
            var itemHeight = item.outerHeight();

            if (itemOffsetTop < 0) {
                // Scroll up to make the item visible at the top
                dropdownContent.scrollTop(scrollTop + itemOffsetTop);
            } else if (itemOffsetTop + itemHeight > dropdownHeight) {
                // Scroll down to make the item visible at the bottom
                dropdownContent.scrollTop(scrollTop + (itemOffsetTop - dropdownHeight + itemHeight));
            }
        }
    });

    $(window).on('load', function () {
        $('#filterByLabel').trigger('click');
    });
</script>
